Generate trial vectors using L-SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n            for i in range(n):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\n                    F_i = 1.0\n                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\n                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\n                F_i_values[i] = F_i\n                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\n                pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\n                pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\n                \n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\n                \n                # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\n                while r1 == i:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\n                candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(n) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                if len(archive) > 0:\n                    candidate_r2 += [n + j for j in range(len(archive))]\n                if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(n))\n                    x2 = pop[r2]\n                else:\n                    r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\n                    if r2 >= n:\n                        x2 = archive[r2 - n]\n                    else:\n                        x2 = pop[r2]\n                \n                mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n            for i in range(n):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    48\t    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    49\t    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    50\t    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n    51\t        self.w_start = w_start\n    52\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    53\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    54\t\n    55\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    56\t        self.it = 0\n    57\t\n    58\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    59\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    60\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    61\t        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n    62\t        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    63\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    64\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    65\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    66\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n    67\t        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n    68\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    69\t\n    70\t\n    71\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    72\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE/L-SHADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    73\t\n    74\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    75\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    76\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    77\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    78\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    79\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling.\n    80\t    - Returns an additional pbest_p hint for current-to-pbest mutations.\n    81\t    \"\"\"\n    82\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    83\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    84\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    85\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    86\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    87\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    88\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    89\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    90\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    91\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    92\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    93\t\n    94\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    95\t        self.it = 0\n    96\t        self.last_gbest = None\n    97\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n    98\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n    99\t\n   100\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   101\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   102\t        # Estimate number of generations; assume roughly pop_size evals per generation\n   103\t        pop_size = int(observation.get('pop_size', 40))\n   104\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', 10000))\n   105\t        max_iter = max(1, int(max_evals / max(1, pop_size)))\n   106\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Smooth cosine decay: gentler in the middle, more aggressive at ends\n   109\t        phase = np.pi * t\n   110\t        F = self.f_start * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(phase)) + self.f_end * (0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(phase))\n   111\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1.0 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   112\t\n   113\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   114\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   115\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   116\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   117\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   118\t            else:\n   119\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   120\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   121\t\n   122\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   123\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   124\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   126\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   127\t\n   128\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   129\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   130\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   131\t        else:\n   132\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   133\t\n   134\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   135\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   136\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   137\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   138\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   139\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   140\t\n   141\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   142\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   143\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   144\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   145\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   146\t\n   147\t        # pbest_p hint for current-to-pbest mutation (used by L-SHADE style mutators)\n   148\t        pbest_p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - t))\n   149\t\n   150\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   151\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon), 'pbest_p': float(pbest_p)}\n   152\t\n   153\t\n   154\tclass LSHADEHeuristicController(ParamController):\n   155\t    \"\"\"A simple L-SHADE-friendly controller.\n   156\t\n   157\t    Shrinks F and CR over the run, boosts diversity on stagnation,\n   158\t    and provides pbest_p / linear_reduction hints for the optimizer.\n   159\t    \"\"\"\n   160\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.85, f_end=0.25, cr_start=0.95, cr_end=0.45,\n   161\t                 stagnation_boost=0.25):\n   162\t        self.f_start = f_start\n   163\t        self.f_end = f_end\n   164\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n   165\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n   166\t     self.stagnation_count = 0\n   172\t\n   173\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   174\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   175\t 178\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   179\t\n   180\t        F = self.f_start + (self.f_end - self.f_start) * t\n   181\t        CR = self.cr_start + (self.cr_end - self.cr_start) * t\n   182\t\n   183\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   184\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   185\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   186\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   187\t            else:\n   188\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   189\t        self.last_gbest = 9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    83\t\n    84\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    85\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    86\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    87\t        \n    88\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    89\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    90\t\n    91\t        # Initialize population - Latin Hypercube Sampling for better coverage\n    92\t        if pop_size > 1:\n    93\t            lhs = np.zeros((pop_size, self.dim))\n    94\t            for j in range(self.dim):\n    95\t                perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n    96\t                lhs[:, j] = (perm + rng.random(pop_size)) / pop_size\n    97\t            pop = lb + lhs * (ub - lb)\n    98\t        else:\n    99\t            pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   100\t        \n   101\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   102\t        evals = 0\n   103\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   104\t        \n   105\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   106\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   107\t                break\n   108\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   109\t            evals += 1\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Track best\n   112\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   113\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   114\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   115\t        \n   116\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   117\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   118\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   119\t\n   120\t        # Main loop\n   121\t        iteration = 0\n   122\t        done = False\n   123\t        \n   124\t        # L-SHADE (Success-History Adaptive Differential Evolution) state\n   125\t        memory_size = 10\n   126\t        memory_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n   127\t        memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n   128\t        memory_idx = 0\n   129\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   130\t        archive_capacity = max(2 * pop_size, 40)\n   131\t        stagnation_count = 0\n   132\t        current_n = pop_size\n   133\t        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.15 * pop_size))\n   134\t        \n   135\t        while not done:\n   136\t            iteration += 1\n   137\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   138\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   139\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   140\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals,\n   141\t                       'pop_size': current_n}\n   142\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   143\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   144\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   145\t            \n   146\t            # Optional controller override (still supported, but L-SHADE also adapts internally)\n   147\t            ctrl_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   148\t            ctrl_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   149\t            \n   150\t            progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, self.max_evals))\n   151\t            # More exploitation as the budget is consumed\n   152\t            pbest_p = float(params.get('pbest_p', max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))))\n   153\t            pbest_p = max(0.05, min(0.6, pbest_p))\n   154\t            \n   155\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   156\t            n = current_n\n   157\t            pop_old = pop[:n].copy()\n   158\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop[:n])\n   159\t            S_F = []\n   160\t            S_CR = []\n   161\t            S_delta = []\n   162\t            F_i_values = np.zeros(n)\n   163\t            CR_i_values = np.zeros(n)\n   164\t            \n   165\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness[:n])\n   166\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(n * pbest_p))\n   167\t            pbest_indices = sorted_idx[:pbest_num]\n   168\t            \n   169\t            # Generate trial vectors using L-SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n   170\t            for i in range(n):\n   171\t                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n   172\t                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n   173\t                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n   174\t                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n   175\t                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n   176\t                if F_i <= 0.0:\n   177\t                    F_i = 0.1\n   178\t                elif F_i > 1.0:\n   179\t                    F_i = 1.0\n   180\t                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\n   181\t                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\n   182\t                F_i_values[i] = F_i\n   183\t                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\n   184\t                \n   185\t                # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\n   186\t                pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\n   187\t                pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\n   188\t                \n   189\t                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   190\t                base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\n   191\t                \n   192\t                # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\n   193\t                r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\n   194\t                while r1 == i:\n   195\t                    r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\n   196\t                candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(n) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n   197\t                if len(archive) > 0:\n   198\t                    candidate_r2 += [n + j for j in range(len(archive))]\n   199\t                if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\n   200\t                    r2 = int(rng.randint(n))\n   201\t                    x2 = pop[r2]\n   202\t                else:\n   203\t                    r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\n   204\t                    if r2 >= n:\n   205\t                        x2 = archive[r2 - n]\n   206\t                    else:\n   207\t                        x2 = pop[r2]\n   208\t                \n   209\t                mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\n   210\t                \n   211\t                # Binomial crossover\n   212\t                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n   213\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n   214\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   215\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   216\t                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   217\t                new_pop[i] = trial\n   218\t            \n   219\t            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n   220\t            for i in range(n):\n   221\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   222\t                    done = True\n   223\t                    break\n   224\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   225\t                evals += 1\n   226\t                \n   227\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   228\t                    # Add previous position to archive before replacement\n   229\t                    old_x = pop_old[i].reshape(1, -1)\n   230\t                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n   231\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, old_x])\n   232\t                    else:\n   233\t                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(archive_capacity))\n   234\t                        archive[replace_idx] = old_x\n   235\t                    \n   236\t                    delta_f = fitness[i] - trial_f\n   237\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   238\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   239\t                    S_F.append(F_i_values[i])\n   240\t                    S_CR.append(CR_i_values[i])\n   241\t                    S_delta.append(delta_f)\n   242\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   243\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   244\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   245\t            \n   246\t            # Update L-SHADE memory using weighted Lehmer mean for F and weighted mean for CR\n   247\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   248\t                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F, dtype=float)\n   249\t                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR, dtype=float)\n   250\t                S_delta_arr = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n   251\t                total_delta = float(np.sum(S_delta_arr))\n   252\t                if total_delta > 1e-30:\n   253\t                    weights = S_delta_arr / total_delta\n   254\t                else:\n   255\t                    weights = np.ones_like(S_delta_arr) / len(S_delta_arr)\n   256\t                if np.sum(weights * S_F_arr) > 1e-30:\n   257\t                    mean_F = float(np.sum(weights * S_F_arr**2) / np.sum(weights * S_F_arr))\n   258\t                else:\n   259\t                    mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n   260\t                mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * S_CR_arr))\n   261\t                memory_F[memory_idx] = max(0.1, min(1.0, mean_F))\n   262\t                memory_CR[memory_idx] = max(0.0, min(1.0, mean_CR))\n   263\t                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % memory_size\n   264\t            \n   265\t            # Linear population size reduction (L-SHADE)\n   266\t            if current_n > min_pop_size:\n   267\t                target_n = max(min_pop_size, int(round(pop_size + (min_pop_size - pop_size) * progress)))\n   268\t                if target_n < current_n:\n   269\t                    remove_count = current_n - target_n\n   270\t                    # Remove worst individuals\n   271\t                    worst_indices = np.argsort(fitness[:current_n])[::-1][:remove_count]\n   272\t                    keep_indices = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(current_n), worst_indices)\n   273\t                    n_keep = len(keep_indices)\n   274\t                    if n_keep > 0:\n   275\t                        pop[:n_keep] = pop[keep_indices]\n   276\t                        fitness[:n_keep] = fitness[keep_indices]\n   277\t                    # Fill remaining slots with best individuals to keep array size\n   278\t                    if n_keep < target_n:\n   279\t                        local_best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness[:max(1, n_keep)]))\n   280\t                        for j in range(n_keep, target_n):\n   281\t                            pop[j] = pop[local_best_idx].copy()\n   282\t                            fitness[j] = fitness[local_best_idx]\n   283\t                    current_n = target_n\n   284\t            \n   285\t            # Stagnation detection: restart worst individuals if no improvement for several iterations\n   286\t            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n   287\t                stagnation_count = 0\n   288\t            else:\n   289\t                stagnation_count += 1\n   290\t            \n   291\t            if stagnation_count >= 5:\n   292\t                n_restart = max(1, int(current_n * 0.15))\n   293\t                worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness[:current_n])[-n_restart:]\n   294\t                sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\n   295\t                for idx in worst_idx:\n   296\t                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   297\t                        break\n   298\t                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n   299\t                        pop[idx] = np.clip(gbest + sigma * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n   300\t                    else:\n   301\t                        pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\n   302\t                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\n   303\t                    evals += 1\n   304\t                    if fitness[idx] < gbest_f:\n   305\t                        gbest_f = fitness[idx]\n   306\t                        gbest = pop[idx].copy()\n   307\t                stagnation_count = 0\n   308\t            \n   309\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   310\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   311\t            \n   312\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   313\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   314\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   315\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   316\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   317\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   318\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   319\t            \n   320\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   321\t\n   322\t        self.stats = {\n   323\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   324\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   325\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   326\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   327\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   328\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   329\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   330\t        }\n   331\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\n     5\tobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\n     6\tadjustment (F, CR).\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\t\n     9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    83\t\n    84\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    85\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    86\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    87\t        \n    88\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    89\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    90\t\n    91\t        # Initialize population - Latin Hypercube Sampling for better coverage\n    92\t        if pop_size > 1:\n    93\t            lhs = np.zeros((pop_size, self.dim))\n    94\t            for j in range(self.dim):\n    95\t                perm = rng.permutation(pop_size)\n    96\t                lhs[:, j] = (perm + rng.random(pop_size)) / pop_size\n    97\t            pop = lb + lhs * (ub - lb)\n    98\t        else:\n    99\t            pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   100\t        \n   101\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   102\t        evals = 0\n   103\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   104\t        \n   105\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   106\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   107\t                break\n   108\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   109\t            evals += 1\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Track best\n   112\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   113\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   114\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   115\t        \n   116\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   117\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   118\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   119\t\n   120\t        # Main loop\n   121\t        iteration = 0\n   122\t        done = False\n   123\t        \n   124\t        # L-SHADE (Success-History Adaptive Differential Evolution) state\n   125\t        memory_size = 10\n   126\t        memory_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n   127\t        memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n   128\t        memory_idx = 0\n   129\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   130\t        archive_capacity = max(2 * pop_size, 40)\n   131\t        stagnation_count = 0\n   132\t        current_n = pop_size\n   133\t        min_pop_size = max(4, int(0.15 * pop_size))\n   134\t        \n   135\t        while not done:\n   136\t            iteration += 1\n   137\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   138\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   139\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   140\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals,\n   141\t                       'pop_size': current_n}\n   142\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   143\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   144\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   145\t            \n   146\t            # Optional controller override (still Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

progress) + 0.05\\n            use_exp_crossover_p = 0.5 * (1.0 - progress)\\n            for i in range(n):\\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\\n                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\\n                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\\n                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\\n                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = 0.1\\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\\n                    F_i = 1.0\\n                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\\n                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\\n                F_i_values[i] = F_i\\n                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\\n\\n                if rng.random() < use_rand1_p:\\n                    # rand/1 mutation: pure exploration\\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    while r1 == i:\\n                        r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    while r2 == i or r2 == r1:\\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    r3 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    while r3 == i or r3 == r1 or r3 == r2:\\n                        r3 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    mutant = pop[r1] + F_i * (pop[r2] - pop[r3])\\n                else:\\n                    # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\\n                    pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\\n                    pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\\n\\n                    # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                    base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\\n\\n                    # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    while r1 == i:\\n                        r1 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                    candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(n) if idx != i and idx != r1]\\n                    if len(archive) > 0:\\n                        candidate_r2 += [n + j for j in range(len(archive))]\\n                    if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\\n                        r2 = int(rng.randint(n))\\n                        x2 = pop[r2]\\n                    else:\\n                        r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\\n                        if r2 >= n:\\n                            x2 = archive[r2 - n]\\n                        else:\\n                            x2 = pop[r2]\\n\\n                    mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\\n\\n                # Binomial or exponential crossover\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\\n                if rng.random() < use_exp_crossover_p:\\n                    # Exponential crossover: copy consecutive genes from mutant\\n                    trial = pop[i].copy()\\n                    L = 0\\n                    while L < self.dim and rng.random() < CR_i:\\n                        trial[(j_rand + L) % self.dim] = mutant[(j_rand + L) % self.dim]\\n                        L += 1\\n                    if L == 0:\\n                        trial[j_rand] = mutant[j_rand]\\n                else:\\n                    mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                    mask[j_rand] = True\\n                    trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\\n                new_pop[i] = trial\"\n    }\n}\n</json>"
